# Agents · H · part 2

922 entries, harness-codebase-researcher to heap-allocation-scanner. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **harness-codebase-researcher**: Investigate existing code, architecture, behavior, dependencies, and risks before a non-trivial implementation decision. Use when raw exploration would consume the main context. Return evidence; do not implement. ([AlperenEvci/claude-code-development-harness](https://github.com/AlperenEvci/claude-code-development-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/harness-codebase-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **harness-coder**: Implementation agent for {project_name} — writes code that follows the project's Espalier rules, layer specs, and Solution Selection Ladder (conventions first, correctness within them, clarity then brevity break ties). Spawned by the pipeline at Stage 3 (implementation — under folded test-mode this includes writing the change's interface/failure-mode tests with the code), re-spawned on review fix rounds (where it fixes the defect CLASS, not the flagged line — see Fix Rounds), and run in CONTRACT PHASE mode for contracted security abuse tests. One task at a time; never reviews its own code. ([Junhanliu-dev/espalier-engineering](https://github.com/Junhanliu-dev/espalier-engineering/blob/HEAD/skills/espalier-init/templates/agents/harness-coder.md) · ★69)
- **harness-coder**: Implements one task of an approved plan until its tests pass, for the harness workflow. Never edits tests. ([cristianradupop/shareTrip](https://github.com/cristianradupop/shareTrip/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/harness-coder.md) · 2 copies)
- **harness-codex**: Researches Codex CLI (harness) in its sources of truth and keeps the spec docs/externos/harness/harness-codex.md current, filing gap issues. Never changes product code. ([patrickbrandao/thinker](https://github.com/patrickbrandao/thinker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/harness-codex.md) · ★6)
- **harness-codex-evaluator**: Adversarial code reviewer for WorldSim's harness pipeline. Runs in Codex (separate model session from the Generator) for bias isolation. Issues APPROVE, RE-CODE, RE-PLAN, or FAIL verdicts. Receives plan, test results, test code, and visual evidence. CAN execute commands (cargo test, grep) — MUST verify claims independently. Does NOT modify production code. Only reads and runs tests. ([hyunlord/new-world](https://github.com/hyunlord/new-world/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/harness-codex-evaluator.md))
- **harness-coding-agent**: Incremental coding agent for one session of a long-running build. Reads claude-progress.txt and feature_list.json to orient itself, selects the next batch of failing features, implements them one at a time, verifies each, commits per-feature, and updates the progress log. Designed to run safely across context resets — each session is fully self-contained. ([bobmaertz/prompt-library](https://github.com/bobmaertz/prompt-library/blob/HEAD/plugins/harness/agents/harness-coding-agent.md))
- **harness-compliance-reviewer**: Review a cli-web-* CLI for convention compliance against skills/shared/CONVENTIONS.md. Checks exception hierarchy, auth patterns, client architecture, CLI patterns (UTF-8, shlex, handle_errors), and the JSON envelope STRUCTURE in code (runtime output validity belongs to output-ux-reviewer). Returns scored findings. Use during Phase 4 standards review. ([ItamarZand88/CLI-Anything-WEB](https://github.com/ItamarZand88/CLI-Anything-WEB/blob/HEAD/cli-anything-web-plugin/agents/harness-compliance-reviewer.md) · ★242)
- **harness-composer**: Evalúa y contrasta trazas de sondeo de múltiples candidatos de arnés, seleccionando la configuración óptima sin depender de ground-truth. ([Fresias/gozster](https://github.com/Fresias/gozster/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/harness-composer.md) · 4 copies)
- **harness-composition**: 하네스는 실행 직전에 prompt, Project 규칙, Skill, tool binding을 하나의 immutable snapshot으로 조립한다. 이것은 권한 강제 장치가 아니며, 권한은 [보안 모델](../../security/control-plane-security-model.md), 실행 경계는 [실행 격리](execution-isolation.md)가 강제한다. ([yarang/grok-fleet-orchestrator](https://github.com/yarang/grok-fleet-orchestrator/blob/HEAD/docs/architecture/agents/harness-composition.md))

Merges agent roster design and command set design in one context. Runs in its own context window. ([Mike-Byrne-AI/espalier-harness](https://github.com/Mike-Byrne-AI/espalier-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/harness-config-advisor.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)

- **harness-gardener**: Deletes unused harness. An unexercised harness rots — a rule nobody reads, a hook that never fires and a skill nobody invokes all keep being loaded, keep costing context, and keep being followed confidently after they stop being true, which is worse than having no harness at all. So deletion is the point of this agent, not a side effect. Delegate ON EXPLICIT REQUEST ONLY — the monthly harness review, or "what harness can we drop", or when CLAUDE.md has grown past its line budget. It reads git log and the transcript history to find rules, skills, agents, hooks, Makefile targets and pre-commit… ([alexeiveselov92/fluentkit-legacy](https://github.com/alexeiveselov92/fluentkit-legacy/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/harness-gardener.md) · ★1)
